7～9 月期実質GDP は年率＋1.0％と 
プラス成長を予想（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比＋0.2％（年率＋1.0％）と、4～6月期（前期比＋
0.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半期連続でのプラス成長を予想 
○ 個人消費が伸び悩む一方、設備投資や民間在庫が押し上げる見込み。なお、公的在庫と輸入では
中東情勢を主因とする一時的な変動が生じ、成長率にプラス・マイナス両面で影響 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）はプラス成長 を
予想 
11月16日 （月） に、 内閣府から2026年7～9月期の 「四半期別GDP速 報（QE）」
（1次速報値）が公表される。現時点で入手可能な基礎統計の結果を踏まえ
ると、7～9月期の実質GDPは前期比＋0.2％（ 年 率＋1.0％） となる見込みだ。
4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半
期連続でプラス成長になると予想する（図表）。 
7～9月期は、内需の柱である個人消費が伸び悩む一方、 設備投資の拡大や、
製造業を中心とする在庫積み増しが成長率を押し上げるとみられる。その他
では、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要因として現れる
が、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイナス要因になるなど、 
図表 2026 年7～9 月期GDP（1 次速報）予測

前期比に対する寄与度（％Pt）

### Page 2

2

中東情勢による一時的な変動も引き続き成長率に影響する見込みだ。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
個人消費は伸び悩み 。耐
久財の特需剥落や、 根強
い節約志向が影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％（4～6月期：同＋0.0％） と、2四半期連続で伸び
悩む結果になろう。消費の弱さの背景には、 複合的な要因があると考えられ
る。 
耐久財では、自動車とエアコンの「特需」剥落が押し下げ要因になろう。
自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に廃止されたこと
を受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていたが、8月以降は再
び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年4月の省エネ基準
引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要が生じていたが、
景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入った後は一服した
模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減少もエアコンなど
季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
食品やエネルギー、 各種消耗品などの非耐久財では、物価高を背景とする
根強い節約志向により、 弱含みが続いているようだ。今回のインフレ局面が
本格化する前の2020年と比較すると、非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6
年半でおよそ1.3倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上
昇が、 消費者の体感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、 購入数量の抑制
につながっている可能性がある。 
サービス消費は、 イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、 サービスや価格帯、 顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。 また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・ 宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、 レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投 資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
設備投資は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.9％） と、3四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスへ転じると予想する。 
直近の設備投資関連指標をみると、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財
総供給指数（除く輸送機械）が7～8月に大きく上昇した。また第3次産業活
動指数では、ソフトウェア業の実質売上高が同じく7～8月に増加したことが
確認でき、どちらも7～9月期の設備投資が拡大したことを示唆している。 
加えて、前期の4～6月期に発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特許権の海外
譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少） がなくなったこともプラス要因だ。た
だし、 これは裏を返せば、7～9月期のQEにおける設備投資の伸びが実態以上
に強い数字になるということでもあり、評価する際には留意が必要である。 
住宅投資は 前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.8％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.6％ ）と、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスとなろう。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～8月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上

### Page 3

3

昇による需要低迷に加えて、 足元では地震や台風などによる着工 ・ 進捗の遅
れが発生している可能性がある。 
民間 在庫は 2四半期連続
で成長率にプラス寄与 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、＋0.2％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラスが続く見込みだ。AI関連需要の強さなどを背景に、製
造業で在庫積み増しの動きが進んでいることが影響すると考えられる。な
お、4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を
押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足
が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響は一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要 は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラスに 
公的需要は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.8％）と、2四半期ぶりの前期
比プラスを予想する。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じるとみられることだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変
動の寄与度が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備
蓄の減少が小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公
的在庫の減少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.3％Pt程度のプラ
ス寄与になると見込まれる。 
その他の公的需要項目では、政府消費を前期比＋0.2％（4～6月期：同＋
1.7％）と予想する。4～6月期は高校授業料・小学校給食費の無償化開始に
より高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした特殊要因が剥落し、緩やかな
増加ペースに戻ると考えられる。公共投資は前期比▲0.5％（4～6月期：同
▲0.5％）と、土木関連工事の減少を受けて2四半期連続のマイナスを見込む。 
輸出が小幅に減少する 一
方、 輸入が増加し、外需は
4四半期ぶりのマイナス
寄与 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.3％（4～6月期：同＋0.4％）と小幅に減少す
る一方、輸 入が同＋1.3％（4～6月 期：同▲1.7％）と増加に転じる見込みだ。
実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.3％Ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナ
スが予想される。 
輸入の増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化により4
～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホルム
ズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの輸入
量が確保されている。 
輸入はGDPの控除項目であることから、7～9月期は輸入増加が成長率の下
押し要因になる。 ただし、代替調達の進展に伴って国家備蓄原油の放出が抑
制され、 先述したように7～9月期は公的在庫変動が成長率にプラス寄与する
とみられることから、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果の一部は公的在庫変
動により相殺されるだろう。 
輸出では、前期の4～6月期に特許権の海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げ
た （これは4～6月期の設備投資の押し下げ要因でもある） 反動が現れ、 一時
的に下振れするだろう。一方、旺盛なAI関連需要が支えになり、半導体・電

### Page 4

4

子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸出は堅調に推移する見込みだ。インバウ
ンド需要 （非居住者家計の国内での直接購入） は、 日中対立の継続による中
国人訪日客の少なさに加え、 航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の伸び悩みに
より、低調な結果になると見込まれる。 
2026 年度後半 の実質 GDP
は小幅プラス と予想 。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期から減速するものの、4四半期
連続でプラス成長になると予想する。GDPの約半分を占める個人消費の伸び
悩みが足を引っ張っているが、設備投資や製造業を中心とする在庫積み増し
がけん引する格好だ。 
先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～2027年3月）の 実質GDP
は、 均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。個人消費が振るわな
い一方で、 設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろう。2026年度全体の
実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平均水準が押し上げら
れることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％程度とまずまずの
成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。 原油 ・ ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、 コア消費者物価指数 （生鮮食品を除く総合） は年度末にかけて前年比＋
3％程度まで上昇すると予想される（ただし、2026年末から政府の電気・ガ
ス料金支援策が実施されれば、0.4～0.5％Pt程度の押し下げ要因になる可能
性がある）。加えて、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを
中心とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、 一時的に消費が落ち込む可能
性がある。 
他方で、年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調
査） では、2026年度の設備投資計画 （ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投
資額 （除く土地投資額）） が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した （6月 調 査：
同＋8.8％）。生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、
設備の不足感が強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、 設備投資は今後拡大
傾向で推移していく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」 （特定生産性向上
設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、 日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、 企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、 また、 投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、 中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。

### Page 5

5

日銀は10月会合で 政策金
利を据え置き 。次回利上
げは12月と予想 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、 日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％） は、次回12
月17・18日の会合と予想する。 前回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて 「3カ月
に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、 日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の 「半年強
に1回」から加速していることを印象付けるものになろう。 
企業の価格転嫁行動は一段と積極化しており、植田総裁が9月会合後の記
者会見で 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明したように、日本銀行は「物価引
き上げ」 から 「物価上振れ抑制」 へと金融政策運営の舵を切っている。12月
の追加利上げ後も、 金利上昇の影響を確認しながら、 日銀は最終的に2027年
半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％まで引き上げていくだろう。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。 
（https://www.mizuhobank.co.jp/corporate/mhri/research/report/pdf/outlook261002.pdf）

## Document 108: 261027_ubs_faraday

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_ubs_faraday.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Faraday Technology
New opportunities in advanced nodes
Q326 beat on turnkey strength and non-op gains; Q426 outlook positive
Q326 EPS of NT$1.07 came in above UBS/Street expectations mainly on  non-op gains.
Q326 sales came in at NT$3.66 bn (+11% QoQ), ahead of its original guidance but in
line with its comment  during Taiwan Summit with turnkey sales up 20% QoQ given
better support from OSAT and foundries, despite NRE declining 31% QoQ. Q326 GM

decreased 2.7ppt QoQ to 42.5% on higher turnkey mix, while  OPEX grew 9% QoQ  on
salary adjustment and higher bonus accrual. Non-op gain of NT$266mn mainly includes
valuation gains on an investee company. For Q426F, it guided sales  flat  QoQ remaining
at high levels with IP and NRE up QoQ, and turnkey down QoQ. GM is guided back to
~45%, reflecting normalized ASIC economics; OPEX should decline single digit QoQ.
2027 growth outlook supported by AI, advanced packaging and 12nm node

Management guided for double-digit sales growth in 2027, with all three businesses, IP ,
NRE and turnkey, expected to grow, driven by three key catalysts: AI, advanced
packaging and new UMC 12nm project contribution. For IP , growth should be
supported by higher royalties on foundry price hikes/customer capacity expansion and
initial  contribution from 12nm node; NRE should benefit from 2nm/4nm projects
entering peak recognition, with 3-4 ASIC tape-outs targeted in H127; while MP should

see growth in both MCU and ASIC as new projects gradually ramp. AI-related mix was at
8-9% of Q326 sales, and is expected to increase throughout 2027 alongside
accelerating advanced-packaging shipments. We expect 2027E sales to grow 37% YoY.
2nm design win opens new advanced-node and product opportunities
Faraday secured a 2nm GAA special purpose  ASIC project worth several tens of millions
of US dollars in NRE, which management described as its largest-ever design win and

most advanced project to date. The project spans front-end design and IP integration,
back-end design, customized 3D DRAM and advanced packaging, and marks Faraday's
first customized 3D DRAM and hybrid-bonding project. It expects to recognize its first
NRE milestone  in H127, followed by MP targeted within 1-2 years. Beyond 2nm, Faraday
is expanding into new applications including OCS, CXL and Si-cap, leveraging its
capabilities in advanced packaging, interposer design and ASIC integration. We believe

successful execution by Faraday and its foundry partner could strengthen Faraday's
positioning in advanced-node projects and create follow-on design-win opportunities.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$200, stay Neutral
We raise    2026E EPS (reported) from NT$3.43 to NT$3.68 on better Q326, but lower
2027/28E EPS by 4%/1% to factor in softer CE demand and higher OPEX. We raise our
PT to NT$200 from NT$190 using 24x 2027E PE (vs prior 22x). We raise our multiple as

the recent 2nm project win should boost investor confidence, but stay Neutral as the
advanced nodes (4nm/2nm) turnkey revenue recognition depends on the foundry
partner's execution. We await clearer evidence before revisiting our assumptions.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation 4.4%

Forecast stock return 5.4%

Forecast excess return -1.0%
Company Description
Faraday was founded in 1993, spun off from UMC's IP and semiconductor design service
department as the first IC design service company in Asia. It is an IC design and production
service provider that initially focused on specialty applications on mature nodes by partnering

with UMC and gradually upgraded to advanced nodes through working with Samsung. The
company had its IPO on the Taiwan Securities Exchange in 1999.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Faraday using PE methodology.
Downside risks include slower-than-expected advanced nodes migration, further delay of
advanced packaging ramp, and growing competition in specialty projects from China IC
design service companies.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 152: 261031_nmr_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261031_nmr_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-31
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

EQUITY: FABLESS
MediaTek   2454.TW 2454 TT
Global Markets Research
30 October 2026
Dispels recent market noises with upside surprise; retain Buy
Quick Note
Maintain Buy and TP of TWD5,800
We recently noted that MediaTek’s earnings call could prove to be a positive catalyst for 
its share price, considering a number of unfounded concerns and noises had taken center 
stage before the call (Preview: market concerns overdone ). However, we believe some of 
MTK’s messages during earnings call turned out to be even more bullish than we had 
expected, including: (1) TPU v.8 contribution of USD2.5bn in 4Q26E (vs. previous 
guidance of more than USD2bn , and Street concerns around MTK potentially missing the 
USD2bn target); (2) management now expects ASIC sales in 2027E to reach the high end 
(USD16bn) of its prior guidance range (USD14-16bn) vs. our forecast of USD14bn; (3) 
TPU v.9 schedule is on track for 1Q28E mass production (a powerful clarification vs. 
Street expectation of 1-2 quarters’ delay); (4) management is very confident about v.9 
EMIB-T future yield trend after resolving 2-3 key issues (implying no CoWoS back-up plan, 
vs. Street expectation of a CoWoS-based v.9 project being opened); (5) Mediatek 
announced that it has secured a 2nd ASIC customer with sales contribution from 2028E; 
(6) ASIC-driven operating leverage will kick in from 4Q26E (guidance suggesting 19% 
OPM in 4Q26E, vs our estimate of 18.6% and 3Q26’s 16%; and management expects 
2027E OPM to be above 20%, vs our estimate of 22%). The only downside surprise might 
be its 4Q26E sales guidance, which suggests 25%-32% q-q growth vs. our estimate of 
34% q-q. We would watch if this is a low bar for MediaTek to beat. GPM guidance of 
45%+-1.5% is 1pp lower than previous quarter’s range (46%+-1.5%; and 46.3% actual in 
3Q26) due to ASIC — in line with our forecast of 45.4%. Overall, we expect the share 
price to react positively to the earnings call and, thus, reiterate our Buy rating and 
TWD5,800 TP, based on 25x unchanged 2027-28 EPS estimate. The stock is currently 
trading at 16.9x.

Fig. 1: MediaTek's 3Q26 results and 4Q26F guidance vs Nomura forecasts and Bloomberg consensus

### Page 2

MediaTek: 3Q26 analysts briefing summary
Below comments are from MediaTek’s management, unless otherwise stated :
Results summary
3Q26 revenue was TWD168.1bn (+10.4% q-q, +18.3% y-y), above the high end of
the TWD152.2–159.8bn guidance.
The beat came mainly from better-than-expected customer demand and
improving supply.
○
Growth was driven by volume production of several new Smart Edge projects and
higher DRAM content in TV SoC.
○
•
GM was 46.3% (+0.1pp q-q, -0.2pp y-y), within the 46% ±1.5pp guidance.•

Opex ratio was 30.2% (vs 31.2% in 2Q26 and 30.9% in 3Q25), within the 31% ±2pp
guidance.
•
OPM was 16.1% (+1.1pp q-q, +0.5pp y-y).•
Net income was TWD28.2bn (+14.6% q-q, +10.8% y-y). Net margin was 16.8%
(+0.6pp q-q, -1.1pp y-y).
•
EPS was TWD17.50 (TWD15.28 in 2Q26, TWD15.84 in 3Q25). Non-TIFRS EPS was
TWD17.92.
•
Average FX was TWD31.99/USD (2Q26: 31.60; guidance assumption: 32).•
Prepared remarks
MediaTek completed a USD3.9bn overseas convertible bond offering in 3Q, with

participation from nVidia (NVDA US, Not rated) and Alphabet (GOOGL US, Not
rated).
This strengthens partnerships with key AI infrastructure players and helps secure
capacity for critical components.
○
•
MediaTek continues to make solid progress in both data center and edge devices.
Datacenter ASIC
Current gen: Demand remains very strong. Initial shipments began in
September, and MediaTek is confident of delivering USD2.5bn of revenue in
4Q26.
The 4Q upside comes from robust customer demand. MediaTek has

worked closely with supply chain partners to secure supply, including
substrates.
■
For 2027E, the program is expected to reach the high end of the revenue
target range, about USD16bn, or around 20% of the AI accelerator ASIC
market. This is underpinned by a solid supply outlook, and MediaTek is
working to secure additional capacity to meet demand.
■
•
Next gen: Development is progressing well and is on track for HVM in 1Q28.
The chip is purpose-built for the customer’s next-generation AI

infrastructure. It delivers multiple times higher performance per watt at a
significantly lower TCO.
■
It is expected to strengthen MediaTek’s market position and contribute
substantially to revenue growth.
■
•
Technology: Solid progress continues in 448G SerDes, 3.5D advanced
packaging, and CPO.
•
Broader customer base: MediaTek is expanding beyond silicon into system
and rack solutions. It connects custom xPUs to the NVLink Fusion ecosystem
and also supports UALink.

•
MediaTek continues to expand engagements with several customers.•
○
Edge
The RTX Spark AI PC (N1X), which integrates an nVidia GPU, became
commercially available in mid-October through major PC OEMs. Next-gen
SoC development is already underway.
•
Dimensity 9500 powers the latest Samsung Galaxy AI tablets. This is the third

Any changes in ASIC schedule and milestones?
The 2nd ASIC is on track and on schedule.
MediaTek is aware of market noise about the tape-out schedule. The chip has

multiple dies, and a couple have already taped out.
○

problems are resolved.
MediaTek is working with both Taiwanese and Japanese suppliers.○
•

closely across the entire technology chain.

New ASIC client
A 2nd customer is confirmed, separate from the lead customer.•
The project has kicked off, with first revenue and mass production expected in 2028.•
The customer is not from China.•
Vendor financing for LLM customers?
MediaTek will consider it if necessary and if it is the right customer.•
Returns versus capex have improved over the past six quarters. Management 
believes capex, whether from CSPs or frontier model companies, will generate 
enough revenue and cash flow. Some support may be needed during the transition 
period before cash generation stabilizes.
•
EMIB-T / HBM5 / technology capability and competition
The entry barrier is the ability to integrate multiple technologies into manufacturable, 
scalable products: advanced packaging, HBM5, 448G SerDes, and low power. It is 
not any single technology.
•
Competitors are strong, and MediaTek does not assume it is far ahead. However, it is 
confident it will win its share of the business.
•
NVLink Fusion benefits and customer interest
It is a business model with strong technical content, and it makes it much easier for 
customers to integrate their own designs.
•
The key advantage is much faster time to compute power.•
MediaTek and nVidia are in discussions with several potential customers. Feedback 
is positive, and MediaTek hopes to announce cases in the near term.
•
448G
448G SerDes has already taped out.•
448G is a must going forward. It is an N6 design with very stringent power 
consumption requirements.
•
It will not be on the critical path, and MediaTek will improve interoperability to serve a 
broader customer base.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

營收走勢圖

電子–資訊服務 
藍新資訊(6938.TT) 
維運收入堆疊，AI 與資安接棒成長

研究部初次給予藍新資訊買進投資評等 ， 主要考量 ：(1)企業 AI 應用由
概念驗證走向落地，加上資安設備屆期汰換與金融業大型主機換機，專
案建置需求延續至 2027 年；(2)建置案驗收後轉入長期維護維運 ， 持續
性收入逐年堆疊，墊高獲利基礎；(3)上櫃現金增資擴大資本規模，承接
億元級大型專案的能力提升。綜上所述，研究部預估藍新資訊  2026、
2027 年營收分別為 8.33 億元與 9.55 億元，分別成長 23.1% 與 
14.7% YoY；稅後 EPS 分別為 7.38 元與 8.39 元，分別成長 23.9% 與 
28.3% YoY。藍新資訊以核心系統維運掌握客戶升級需求，並以約九成盈
餘發放現金股利，以 10/16 興櫃收盤價 80.8 元計算，2026 年現金殖
利率約 7.2%。 採樣資訊服務類股本益比介於 10～20 倍 ， 考量藍新資訊 
2026、2027 年獲利維持雙位數成長，以 2027 年 EPS 為評價基礎，給
予區間中緣的 15 倍本益比，目標價 125 元，評等為買進。

1. 核心系統技術門檻高，客戶黏著度強 
藍新資訊以 IBM UNIX 大型主機起家 ， 承接金融核心交易與企業 ERP 系
統，國內具備同等能力的業者有限；建置與資料搬遷不委外原廠，使毛
利率高於一般設備經銷商，主要客戶合作多逾十年。

2. 建置帶動維運，持續性收入逐年堆疊 
核心系統驗收並度過保固期後，客戶多續簽一至三年以上的維護合約，
存續率逾九成 ， 維運收入因此逐年堆疊 ，2025 年以近四成營收貢獻逾五
成毛利 。 研究部預估整合技術與服務 2026、2027 年營收分別成長 13.4% 
與 14.5% YoY。

3. AI 落地與換機需求，上櫃後承接大型專案 
企業 AI 應用由概念驗證走向落地，帶動 AI 相關營收占比由 2025 年
約 3% 升至約 8%，加上資安與大型主機換機，專案需求延續至 2027 
年。上櫃後單案規模可望擴大至 1～2 億元，研究部預估商品銷售與系
統整合合計 2026、2027 年營收分別成長 29.4% 與 14.8% YoY。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 2

公司簡介 
藍新資訊成立於 2014 年，由藍新科技的系統整合事業群分割獨立，2018 年
起獲精誠資訊策略性投資，目前精誠持股約 48%。藍新資訊為資訊服務產業
的系統整合商，向上透過加值代理商取得 IBM、HPE、Dell、Cisco、NetApp、
VMware、Palo Alto 等國際原廠軟硬體，向下直接服務政府機關、金融機構與
大型企業。藍新資訊以 IBM Power 等 UNIX 大型主機平台起家，長期承接企
業 ERP、MES、HIS 與金融核心交易系統的建置與維運，並延伸至儲存備援、
網路資安與 AI 算力平台。

產品結構 
藍新資訊的營收分為商品銷售 、 系統整合 、 整合技術與服務三大業務 。 商品銷
售與系統整合屬專案性收入，提供伺服器、儲存、網路與資安設備的銷售、規
劃與安裝建置，2025 年營收占比分別為 10.3% 與 50.7%；依應用區分，雲
端基礎建設近五成，數據儲存、數位韌性與 AI 應用平台約 25～35%，資訊
安全與網路約 17～25%。整合技術與服務為保固期滿後的例行維護、SLA 支
援、高階維運與人力派駐，屬持續性收入，2025 年營收占比 38.8%，貢獻約 
54% 的毛利 。 另有少量機臺租賃收入 （占比 0.2%） ， 圖一併入商品銷售計算。

圖一、2024～2027F 藍新資訊各業務營收比重

大型企業的核心系統，並延伸至

資訊的獲利核心。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 3

商業模式 
藍新資訊以專案建置取得客戶核心系統，再以長期維護維運把一次性收入轉
為持續性收入 。 專案完成驗收並度過保固期後 ， 客戶多延續簽訂一至三年以上
的維護合約 ； 核心系統對穩定度要求高 ， 更換服務商的風險與成本也高 ，近三
年 平 均 維 護 合 約 存 續 率 逾 九 成 ， 合 約 件 數 2023 ～2025 年複合成長率  
14.48%。 維運過程掌握客戶的 IT 環境 ， 又帶出設備汰換 、 架構升級與資安強
化的新專案 ， 形成建置帶動維運 、 維運帶動升級的循環。 整合技術與服務以同
一組技術團隊服務更多設備與合約 ， 人力成本相對固定 ， 營收占比由 2024 年
的 33.0% 提升至 2025 年的 38.8%，毛利率由 40.7% 升至 43.1%，帶動整
體毛利率由 2024 年的 28.1% 升至 2025 年的 31.0%。

圖二、藍新資訊由專案導入延伸至長期維運

資料來源：藍新資訊，統一投顧整理

營收季節性 
藍新資訊營收具季節性 ， 政府機關與企業多於年底集中驗收結案 ， 第四季為全
年高峰，2023～2025 年第四季營收分別占全年 42.7%、52.2% 與 41.2%，12 
月單月即占全年 25～31%。商品銷售與系統整合於驗收時點認列，單季營收
隨大型專案進度波動 ； 整合技術與服務依合約期間認列 ， 可平滑專案收入的波
動。2026 年 1～9 月營收 5.06 億元（+27.2% YoY），研究部預估 4Q26 營
收 3.26 億元，占全年 39.2%。

建置帶動維運 、 維運帶動升級 ， 維
護合約存續率逾九成，持續性收
入逐年堆疊並推升整體毛利率。

年底驗收結案集中，第四季營收
占全年四至五成；維運收入依合
約認列，平滑專案收入的波動。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

圖三、2023～2026F 藍新資訊各季營收

資料來源：藍新資訊、統一投顧預估

客戶結構與技術能量 
藍新資訊累計服務客戶逾 400 家，營收約 50% 來自民營大型企業，政府機
關約 40%，金融機構約 10%，客戶包括行政院所屬部會、金控與證券業，以及
高科技與製造業龍頭，主要客戶合作多逾十年。客戶分散，2025 年前十大客
戶合計占營收約 35%，單一最大客戶占 7.0%；1Q26 因數位轉型再造建置案
第一階段驗收，單一客戶占比升至 14.3%，屬專案認列時點造成的短期集中。
藍新資訊員工約 135 人，其中技術人員 86 人，累計取得 642 張專業證照，
其中雲端運算、AI 與大數據、資訊安全與  IBM 大型主機四大領域合計占  
93%。建置與資料搬遷由內部團隊執行、不委外原廠，可自行完成  SAP 與 
Oracle ERP 的跨平台移轉，是毛利率高於一般設備經銷商的主因。

AI 應用與資訊安全布局 
藍新資訊成立 AI 智能發展部，提供 GPU 算力平台、向量資料庫、RAG 知識
庫 、 企業私有化大型語言模型部署與資料治理 ， 並以駐點輔導與教育訓練協助
客戶落地，以既有逾 400 家客戶為交叉銷售基礎。單純銷售設備的毛利率有
限 ， 藍新資訊以自有技術團隊提供資料治理 、系統整合與後續維運 ， 聚焦整合
服務而非設備銷售 。 資安業務不與專業資安廠商正面競爭 ， 而是從既有維運客
戶切入，承接防火牆汰換、網路架構升級、零信任身分與存取控管，以及雙活
資料中心與災難備援。上櫃現金增資後，藍新資訊已開始承接 8,000 萬元以
上的案件，未來可承接 1～2 億元等級的大型專案，較過去單案多在 5,000
～6,000 萬元以下明顯擴大。

客戶逾 400 家且分散，建置與資
料搬遷不委外，是藍新資訊毛利
率高於一般設備經銷商的主因。

AI 由伺服器銷售延伸至資料治
理與應用落地，以整合服務支撐
毛利率；資安則從維運切入換機
商機。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

產業分析 
台灣資訊服務市場 
依資策會 MIC 統計與預估，台灣系統整合市場 2023～2027 年複合成長率約 
7.8%，資訊委外市場則達 21.1%，主因企業 IT 架構日趨複雜、資訊人才短
缺，基礎設施維運與資安逐步交由專業廠商承接；台灣資安產值 2025～2027 
年亦可望維持雙位數成長 。 研究部認為委外與資安成長高於系統整合 ， 有利維
運收入占比近四成、並以維運切入資安換機的藍新資訊。

AI 落地與資安換機推升系統整合需求 
企業 AI 應用由概念驗證走向落地，除採購 GPU 伺服器，還需搭配儲存、高
速網路 、 容器平台 、 資料治理與權限控管 ， 導入 AI Agent 後亦須與既有 ERP、
人資及核心交易系統串接；台灣企業的研發與財務等敏感資料多傾向留在自
有機房，私有化 AI 部署更仰賴具備底層整合能力的系統整合商。AI 普及同
時提高資料外洩風險 ， 老舊網路與資安設備又陸續停止原廠支援 ， 政府與企業
須換機並導入零信任架構與雙活備援。核心系統亦進入換機期，金融業  IBM 
大型主機進入汰換階段，企業 ERP 平台也因效能與資安規範展開新一輪升
級。研究部認為AI 落地、資安換機與核心系統汰換同時發生，加上 IT 人才
短缺使企業提高維運委外比重，有利兼具建置與維運能力的系統整合商。

營運概況 
1. AI 基礎設施建置帶動 2Q26 營收年增逾三成 
企業加速建置基礎設施帶動 2Q26 營收達 1.85 億元（+27.3% QoQ 、+32.6% 
YoY） 。惟記憶體與關鍵零組件漲價推升設備採購成本，毛利率降至 29.2%（-
2.0ppt QoQ、-0.8ppt YoY），營益率 12.9%；稅後淨利 1,930 萬元（+5.3% 
QoQ、+118.3% YoY），EPS 1.54 元。

2. 年底驗收旺季帶動 2026 年營收年增逾兩成 
3Q26 營收 1.77 億元（-4.1% QoQ 、+38.2% YoY ），1～9 月累計營收年增 
27.2%。年底為政府與企業驗收結案高峰，政府預算解凍後標案陸續執行，預
估 4Q26 營收 3.26 億元（+84.3% QoQ、+17.1% YoY），惟大型建置案比重
提高，毛利率降至 26.6%，EPS 2.72 元。預估 2026 年營收 8.33 億元
（+23.1% YoY），EPS 7.38 元。

3. AI 與資安需求延續，2027 年營收維持雙位數成長 
展望 2027 年，企業 AI 應用由概念驗證走向落地，AI 相關營收占比已由 
2025 年約 3% 提升至約 8%，部分客戶的 AI 案件已進入概念驗證，並著手
編列 2027 年預算；老舊網路與資安設備陸續停止原廠支援，加上金融業大
型主機進入汰換期，換機需求延續，部分大型專案的認列亦延續至 2027 年。
預估 2027 年營收 9.55 億元 （+14.7% YoY） ，毛利率 29.6%，EPS 8.39 元。

台灣資訊委外與資安市場成長高
於系統整合，有利維運收入占比
近四成的藍新資訊。

AI 落地、資安換機與核心系統汰
換同時發生，系統整合商由銷售
設備轉向提供整體架構與長期維
運。

基礎設施建置帶動 2Q26 營收年
增 32.6%，惟記憶體與零組件漲
價使毛利率降至 29.2%。

年底 驗收旺季加上政府預算解
凍 ， 預估 4Q26 營收年增 17.1%，
2026 年 EPS 7.38 元。

資安與大型主機換機需求延續，
預估 2027 年營收年增 14.7%，
EPS 8.39 元。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

投資建議 
研究部初次給予藍新資訊買進投資評等，主要考量：(1)企業 AI 應用由概念
驗證走向落地 ， 加上資安設備屆期汰換與金融業大型主機換機 ， 專案建置需求
延續至 2027 年；(2)建置案驗收後轉入長期維護維運 ， 持續性收入逐年堆疊 ，
墊高獲利基礎；(3)上櫃現金增資擴大資本規模，承接億元級大型專案的能力
提升。綜上所述，研究部預估藍新資訊 2026、2027 年營收分別為 8.33 億
元與 9.55 億元，分別成長 23.1% 與 14.7% YoY ；稅後 EPS 分別為 7.38 
元與 8.39 元，分別成長 23.9% 與 28.3% YoY。藍新資訊以核心系統維運掌
握客戶升級需求 ， 並以約九成盈餘發放現金股利 ， 以 10/16 興櫃收盤價 80.8 
元計算，2026 年現金殖利率約 7.2%。採樣資訊服務類股本益比介於 10～20 
倍，考量藍新資訊 2026、2027 年獲利維持雙位數成長，以 2027 年 EPS 為
評價基礎，給予區間中緣的 15 倍本益比，目標價 125 元，評等為買進。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

提 供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見，不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其
他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로 
차별화 
HBM4는 예정대로 순항 
중 
HBM 8Hi는 GPU를 더 
팔기 위한 수단 
공장 짓는다고 물량이 
늘어나진 않는다 
26년 주주환원은 100조원 
수준

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

그림 5. SK하이닉스 투자자별 순매수 추이(2010년부터 누계)  그림 6. SK하이닉스 마이크론 밸류에이션 비교

동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

3Q26 전사 예상 영업이익 100조원 
DS부문 예상 영업이익은 100.0조원(OPM 67.4%, YoY +1538.5%, QoQ +14.5%) 으로, 
달러환율 1,380원기준, 메모리와 비메모리 사업부문 예상 영업손익은 각각 102.0조원,    
-2.0조원으로 추정한다. 1) 메모리반도체 가격 상승세가 이어지는 가운데, HBM4 판매 
본격화에 따른 영향을 고려하면 DRAM/NAND 분기 평균 가격 상승폭은 각각 +18.0%, 
+16.0%으로 추정하고, 2)비메모리반도체 사업 적자는 지속될 전망이기 때문이다. 다만 
특별 성과급 지급에 따른 충당금 영향을 제외하고 선단공정 중심의 파운드리 가동률을 
고려하면 적자폭은 유의미하게 감소한 것으로 파악된다는 점이 긍정적이다.   
SDC부문 예상 영업이익은 1.1조원(OPM 13.0%, YoY -9.6%, QoQ +54.9%)으로, 1)국내 
주력 고객사향 폴더블 스마트폰 판매 호조가 사업부의 외형 성장에 긍정적으로 작용했을 
것으로 추정됨에도 불구하고, 2) 8.6세대 IT OLED 패널 양산 개시에 따른 감가상각비(약 
0.3조원 수준)와 북미 고객 중심의 판가 인하 압박 영향으로 전년동기대비 수익성은 약 
2%pt 훼손이 불가피할 전망이다. DX부문 예상 영업손익은 -1.1조원(YoY 적자전환, QoQ 
적자지속)으로, 메모리반도체를 포함한 IT 부품 가격 부담이 전분기대비 확대되고 있기 
때문이다. MX와 VD/DA사업부 예상 손익은 각각 -0.9조원, -0.2조원으로 추정한다.

투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 63만원으로 상향 조정  
메모리반도체의 가격 상승폭이 다소 둔화되겠지만, 2027~2028년에도 타이트한 수급 
환경이 이어질 전망이라는 점을 주목한다. 1)시장은 신규 증설과 HBM 관련 노이즈로 인한 
2028년 피크아웃 가능성을 우려하고 있으나, HBM 의 범용 DRAM 생산능력 잠식과 AI 
추론 확산에 따른 고용량 메모리 수요를 감안하면 공급 제약의 해소 시점은 예상보다 
늦어질 가능성이 높기 때문이다. 뿐만 아니라, 2)메모리 업사이클이 장기화될 경우 동사 
FCF는 과거대비 구조적으로 확대되어 보다 친화적인 주주환원 정책(2027~2029년, 
2027년 1월 예정)을 제시할 가능성도 배제할 수 없기 때문이다. 동사 2027년 연간 실적 
추정치 상향 조정에 따라 목표주가를 63만원(2027년 BPS * Target PBR 3.5배 적용)으로 
상향 조정한다. 피크아웃 우려에 따른 주가 조정은 중장기 관점에서 매수 기회다.

## Document 244: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 파업 영향에도 견조한 실적, 4분기 성장 확대삼성바이오로직스(207940) 파업 영향에도 견조한 실적, 4분기_22fa3b02

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 파업 영향에도 견조한 실적, 4분기 성장 확대삼성바이오로직스(207940) 파업 영향에도 견조한 실적, 4분기_22fa3b02.pdf

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 2,000,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.21) 
1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

## Document 265: AI可以免疫高利率？泡沫末期，市场往往充斥着幻想

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI可以免疫高利率？泡沫末期，市场往往充斥着幻想.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

当分析师⾼喊 " ⾼利率不影响 AI 繁荣 " 时，历史的回响令⼈不安 —— 这与 2000 年互联⽹泡
沫、 2008 年⾦融危机前⼣的叙事如出⼀辙。数据已在⽆声⽰警： Alphabet 、 Meta 、微软等
科技巨头表外承诺总规模达 4.11 万亿美元， 90 天内暴增近 1 万亿；纳斯达克 100 本⽉市值蒸
发逾 1.2 万亿美元。表外承诺不随股价消失， AI 商业化收⼊能否赶在债务浪潮到来前完成追
赶？
摘要：
当债券收益率攀升，分析师们却挥挥⼿说 "AI 繁荣不受影响 "—— 这是经典的泡沫逻辑。历史上每
⼀次重⼤繁荣的终结，⽆不伴随着融资条件的收紧；⽽每⼀次泡沫末期，市场都会涌现出各种
理由，声称 " 这⼀次不同 " 。 AI 热潮能否真正免疫⾼利率，答案或许已在数字中悄然浮现。
以 Alphabet 、 Meta 、微软、亚⻢逊为核⼼的超⼤规模云计算企业及 Nvidia 、 Broadcom 、
SpaceX ，其披露的表外承诺总规模已达 4.11 万亿美元 —— 仅在约 90 天内新增承诺接近 1 万亿美

元。与此同时，这些公司账⾯上的实际债务和租赁负债合计约 1 万亿美元，意味着每 1 美元表内
负债背后，对应着超过 4 美元未在资产负债表上显⽰的承诺。与之形成对照的是，纳斯达克 100
指数成份股本⽉市值合计蒸发逾 1.2 万亿美元。
表外承诺不因股价涨跌⽽消失，到期必须兑付。当前的核⼼悬念在于：AI 商业化收⼊能否在下
⼀轮承诺浪潮到来之前实现追赶。⽽随着⾼利率对 AI ⼦⾏业的冲击逐步显现，市场正在从狂热
⾛向分化，资本配置趋于审慎。
泡沫末期的惯常叙事：⾼利率 " 不重要 "
Rockefeller International 主席 Ruchir Sharma 指出，每当债券收益率上⾏，总有分析师声称 AI 繁
荣可以独善其⾝，⽽这恰恰是经典的泡沫逻辑 —— 资⾦价格永远重要，且有充分理由相信，当
下⽐以往任何时候都更为如此。
2000 年互联⽹泡沫期间，市场流⾏的神话是 " 互联⽹产品的巨⼤需求 " 使科技公司摆脱了利率等
普通经济规律的约束。 2008 年危机前⼣，分析师则试图论证 " 利率上升不会刺破泡沫 " ，或最多

只会放缓⽽⾮逆转资产价格涨势，因为企业盈利处于 "30 年来最佳⽔平 " 。

如今相似的论调以 AI 之名卷⼟重来：⼏个百分点的利率，对于那些正在创造历史性价值增⻓的公
司⽽⾔⽆⾜轻重；美联储以区区基点计量的加息，不可能压制那些志在赢得 AI 军备竞赛的公司的
⽀出意愿。
Sharma 强调，此轮利率环境与 1990 年代的可⽐性有限。当前利率不仅处于⾼位，更是在近 25 年
历史性低利率之后短期内急速攀升 ——这⼀剧烈的机制转换本⾝⾜以造成⾦融压⼒。已有迹象
浮现：投资者要求以更⾼收益率为 AI 供应链中较弱的借款⽅融资。
表外承诺急速膨胀，单季新增逾 5700 亿美元
当前 AI 基础设施投资狂潮的规模，在财务数据中呈现出惊⼈的轮廓。
据最新季报数据，以 Alphabet 、 Meta 、微软、亚⻢逊为核⼼的超⼤规模云计算企业及 Nvidia 、

Broadcom 、 SpaceX ，其披露的表外承诺总规模已达 4.11 万亿美元，较摩根⼠丹利 8 ⽉末统计的
3.12 万亿美元增加近 9920 亿美元 —— 约 90 天内新增承诺接近 1 万亿美元。如果把摩根⼠丹利此前
统计中纳⼊的、基于新闻报道⽽⾮正式财报披露的表外承诺也计算在内，总额将达到 4.29 万亿美
元。
会计处理上的不透明，正在引发直接的市场反应。
分析机构 Needham 指出，⼀旦将表外负债纳⼊计算， Meta 的企业价值将提升 35% 。 Meta 在财报
披露后信⽤违约掉期触及历史⾼位，随即宣布最⾼ 950 亿美元的股权融资计划，其中 375 亿美元

最具变⾰性的科技热潮，同样未必能够抗拒资⾦⾼价的引⼒。

Topics  Loss‑making firms are on the rise in China's growth sectors

In China, the number of companies operating in the red is increasing. And the rise is particularly striking in growth 
sectors. One factor behind the increase is the intense competition among firms, which has led to a situation in which 
only the strongest thrive. Another is that many next‑generation growth companies, despite being viewed as having great 
potential, are losing money.

■ Loss‑making firms are on the rise in growth sectors 
In recent years, the number of companies in China's 
growth sectors that are operating in the red has 
increased markedly. The share of loss-making firms in 
all listed firms (below, "loss‑making‑firm ratio") rose 
sharply from 9.6% in 2019 to 26.9% in 2025. Looking 
at the industry distribution of loss‑making firms, from 
2019 to 2022, the ratio increased mainly in 
non‑manufacturing sectors such as real estate and 
accommodation and eating/drinking services, 
reflecting the property market downturn and strict 
stay‑at‑home measures implemented to curb the spread 
of COVID‑19. Later, from 2022 to 2025, the 
loss‑making-firm ratio surged in 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices; general, production, and 
commercial machinery; electrical machine ry; and 
chemicals. These four sectors alone accounted for 
around 60% of the overall increase. 
They are the principal growth sectors in Chinese 
manufacturing. This is evidenced by the fact that the 
growth rates of value added in all four sectors exceed 
the average for Chinese manufacturing. Many of the 
high‑tech manufacturing industries that the Chi nese 
government has prioritized for development are 
included in these sectors. For example, 5G base 
stations and semiconductors fall under 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices. And robots fall under general, 
production, and com mercial machinery. Meanwhile, 
solar cells and batteries fall under electrical machinery, 
and biopharmaceuticals and organic chemical 
materials fall under chemicals.  
Looking at recent developments in the 
manufacturing of information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices, the global 
communications standard began shifting from 4G 
(LTE) to 5G services in around 2019, and ChatGPT was released in November 2022. These developments 
have provided tailwinds for the growth of the sector. Recently, as it has become clear that ordinary 
consumers and companies can make use of AI services on a daily basis, demand for AI‑related devices and 
semiconductors, as well as d ata centers, has soared. In this environment, many firms are focusing on 
strengthening development, manufacturing, and sales, and the sector's value added has continued to 
increase, rising from 780 billion yuan in 2022 to 910 billion yuan in 2025. Yet over  the same period, the 
number of loss‑making firms increased from 113 to 198, and the loss‑making‑firm ratio jumped from 17.2% 
to 30.1%. 
■ Competition among companies means only the strongest thrive 
One reason loss‑making firms are increasing despite operating in growth sectors is the intense 
competition among companies. The fight for market share has led to overheated investment competition

2 
and price competition. In information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing, 
the rate of growth of non -current assets from 2022 to 
2025 (+35.4%) far exceeded the growth rate of value 
added (+16.9%). This indicates that supply capacity is 
expanding faster than demand. In addition, the 
consumer price index (CPI) increased by just 0.4% 
over the same period. Service prices rose 2.2%, 
reflecting wage increases, while goods prices fell 0.7% 
due to excess supply in manufacturing. 
Information/communications equipment prices 
decreased by 3.5%, larger than the drop for goods 
overall. 
As the selection of the fittest through competition 
among companies has progressed, profits have become 
concentrated at a small number of highly competitive 
firms. The share of profits earned by the top 1% of 
firms in information/communications equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing rose sharply 
from 24.0% in 2022 to 43.3% in 2025. Leading companies are simultaneously establishing positions as 
both "domestic champions" and "global champions."  
■ Next‑generation growth companies are viewed as having great potential even though they 
are losing money 
However, loss‑making firms cannot simply be lumped together and labeled as losers. Globally, investors 
have shown increasing interest recently in companies with high growth potential even if they are operating 
in the red, and China has a considerable number of such firms. 
In information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices manufacturing, one out of 
every two loss‑making firms is a company attracting 
strong investor interest. The Shanghai Composite 
Index rose 28.5% from 3,089 points at the beginning of 
2023 to 3,969 points at the end of 2025. 20 of the 198 
loss‑making firms in 2025 were not yet listed in 2023, 
and if these are excluded, there were 178 companies 
operating in the red as of 2025. Of these 178 firms, 96 
(53.9%) saw stock‑price increases over the period 
2023-2025 that exceeded the rise in the Shanghai 
Composite Index.  
One characteristic of these 96 companies with strong 
relative stock‑price performance is their active 
investment in R&D and intangible assets. Their 
average R&D intensity (R&D investment / sales) in 
2022 was 9.6%, and their ratio of intangible assets to 
value added stood at 60.5%. Intangible assets include 
computer software, patents, copyrights, and 
relationships with customers and suppliers. In contrast, 
the 82 loss‑making firms with weak relative 
stock‑price performance had an R&D intensity of only 
6.5% and an intangible‑asset‑to‑value‑added ratio of just 38.9%. 
As the above has shown, the increase in loss‑making firms in China's growth sectors is notable, and the 
factors behind it include not only the selection of the fittest as a result of competition among companies but 
also the emergence of next‑generation growth firms that are viewed as having great potential. 
  (Shinichi Seki)

Topics  Climate change heightens the risk of growth stagnation in Asia

Climate change could elevate the risk of economic growth flatlining in the ASEAN zone and India by causing labor 
productivity growth and capital accumulation to stall. To mitigate or avert such a scenario, establishing a business 
environment and institutions that promote "adaptation investment" by both the public and private sectors is an urgent 
task.

■ Heavy‑rain and flood damage is becoming increasingly serious in Asia 
Huge downpours and floods 
resulting in human casualties have 
been a frequent occurrence in Asia in 
the past several years. Still fresh in the 
memory is the debris flow that 
occurred in August this year near the 
border between Nepal and China, 
which as of t he time of writing has 
been recognized as a major disaster 
with thousands dead or missing. And 
in November of last year, Southeast 
Asia and South Asia saw 
record‑breaking torrential rains, 
resulting in numerous casualties, 
particularly in Indonesia and Sri  
Lanka. 
One factor that heightens the risks 
of such floods and heavy rains is 
climate change. As temperatures rise, 
the amount of water vapor in the 
atmosphere increases. Therefore, 
global warming could intensify 
torrential rainfall. Although such risk 
is shared a cross Asia, the way its 
effects are felt differs by region. 
Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and 
India are heavily affected by 
monsoons (seasonal winds), with 
many areas experiencing concentrated 
rainfall during the wet season. In such 
areas, warming can furthe r intensify 
monsoon‑driven heavy rains, 
increasing the risks of floods and 
landslides.  
■ Climate change will delay the escape from the "middle‑income trap" 
Climate change not only results in human casualties by way of natural disasters, but may also undermine 
the long‑term economic growth potential of ASEAN and India through various channels. The Asian 
Development Bank (ADB) [2024] conducted a quantitative an alysis of the extent to which climate change 
will weigh on Asian economies over the long term. Based on the IPCC's "very high GHG emissions" 
scenario (SSP5‑8.5), the analysis assumes a 4.7°C rise in the Earth's average temperature by 2100 relative 
to pre-industrial levels, and estimates the difference in real GDP between that and a hypothetical scenario 
without climate change. The results illustrate the long‑term impact on economic growth through declines 
in labor productivity, stagnation in capital accumulation, and downw ard pressure on consumption due to 
sea‑level rise, floods, heat waves, and other phenomena caused by climate change.  
An examination of the results reveals that in every country/territory, the pushdown effect on real GDP 
relative to the no‑climate‑change scenario expands over time, with 2035, 2050, and 2070 each seeing 
greater impact. The pushdown effect for ASEAN and Ind ia is −15.0% to −8.8% in 2050 and −30.2% to

4 
−18.1% in 2070, larger than the effects for China (2050: −4.8%, 2070: −10.3%) and Central Asian countries 
(2050: −1.7%, 2070: −3.8%). Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and India can be considered regions where the 
risk of economic stagnation due to climate change is relatively large.  
This may make it even more difficult for ASEAN countries and India to escape the "middle‑income trap." 
Although their income levels have risen, they still remain far from high‑income status. Indonesia, Malaysia, 
the Philippines, and Thailand became middle‑income countries in the 1990s or earlier, but have still not 
been able to join the groups of high‑income countries. Furthermore, Vietnam and India, which became 
middle‑income countries in the 2000s or later, have yet to enter a high‑growth trajectory like China once 
did. Climate change, by causing labor productivity growth to stall amid rising temperatures and companies 
to curtail investment due to more frequent disasters, may lead to income growth in the ASEAN zone and 
India being delayed further.  
■ Improving the environment to accelerate "adaptation investment" will be important 
To alleviate the pushdown effect of climate change on growth, expansion of "adaptation investment" by 
the public and private sectors is essential. Specifically, this could include improving working environments 
and promoting measures to lighten workloads a nd reduce headcounts in order to offset declines in labor 
productivity caused by rising temperatures, as well as developing infrastructure and strengthening facilities 
to prevent damage to production equipment from sea‑level rise and floods. However, ASEAN  and India 
cannot be said to have sufficiently 
developed the foundations for public 
and private promotion of such 
adaptation investment.  
As part of its Global Adaptation 
Initiative (ND‑GAIN), the University of 
Notre Dame (U.S.) evaluates countries' 
ability to use investment to achieve 
climate‑change adaptation in the form of 
a "readiness" score with three 
components: economic readiness, 
governance readiness, and social 
readiness. Respectively, these 
components assess 1) whether a 
business environment conducive to 
adaptation investment by private -sector 
companies has been put in place, 2) 
whether the effectiveness of institutions

and policies to support private-sector adaptation investment has been secured, and 3) whether tangible and 
intangible social infrastructure that promotes investment has been established. 
Looking at readiness in 2024, ASEAN countries and India generally rank in the middle to lower tiers 
globally, with a large gap between them and developed countries. Notably, the Philippines, India, and 
Thailand fall outside the top 100, indicating weak momentum for adaptation investment. Breaking down 
the scores, problems are especially evident in the economic and governance categories. India and Thailand 
have particularly low eco nomic readiness, ranking 113th and 124th, respectively, and five countries 
(Malaysia not included) fall outside the top 100 for governance readiness. Given this situation, to accelerate 
private‑sector adaptation investment, India and Thailand in particular  need to move swiftly to improve the 
financial environment to make it easier to procure necessary funds domestically and internationally, relax 
regulations to encourage the launch of new businesses, and reform labor markets to enable the smooth 
securing of human resources. In addition, for Asia as a whole, it will also be important to boost confidence 
in the institutional factors that enhance application of investment for adaptation by increasing policy 
stability, ensuring the effectiveness of legal systems, and curbing corruption.

(Tomohiro Hosoi)

## Document 294: Capital Market Outlook (2026-09-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-09-28).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The Five Shocks of 2026—And the Extraordinary Resilience of the U.S. Economy
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
This is the year the consensus got mugged.
Rewind to the start of the year. The market consensus was that the U.S. economy was
set to slow. The Fed was set to cut interest rates. Corporate earnings were set to grow—

but not explode. Energy prices would remain tame. And the odds looked good that the
Republicans would control the House, the Senate, or both, following the midterm election.
Nine months on, the landscape looks starkly different. Defined by five shocks—energy,
interest rates, earnings, growth, and politics—2026 looks nothing like the year investors
thought they were buying on January 1.
Shock 1: Diesel Matters. While global oil prices have moved higher since the start of

the U.S./Iran war on February 28, oil prices have yet to surpass their previous record high
because the world has released some 400 million barrels from strategic reserves this year,
China has curbed its demand for oil imports, and the U.S. has goosed its energy exports to
record highs.
But here is the problem: While gasoline powers the American consumer, diesel powers the
American economy—and diesel prices are now sitting at record highs. Diesel supplies have

been hammered by wars in the Middle East and Ukraine that have sharply reduced refining
capacity. According to the International Energy Agency, by August, exports of diesel from
refiners in the Middle East had fallen by a quarter from prewar levels. Meanwhile, Ukraine
has hit 24 of Russia’s 34 largest refineries, reducing supplies from one of the world’s
largest diesel exporters. The upshot: U.S. diesel prices have soared from $3.55 a gallon
at the start of the year to roughly $6.52 a gallon. While crude oil trades for around $100

a barrel, the retail equivalent of a barrel of diesel now costs more than $260. The diesel
shock of 2026 was the last thing investors saw coming—and remains a key impediment to
structurally lower inflation.
Shock 2: From Interest Rate Cuts to Rate Hikes. Perhaps no other consensus view
entering 2026 has been turned more dramatically on its head than expectations for the
Fed. Heading into 2026, markets were pricing in rate cuts on expectations of a cooling

labor market, moderating inflation, and slower economic growth. The Fed easing cycle was
expected to continue in 2026. In January 2026, futures showed a roughly 50% chance of a
cut by March.
Expectations, however, flipped in late February with the start of the U.S./Iran war. The
Middle East energy shock reignited inflationary pressures, which, combined with stronger-
than-expected U.S. economic growth and a tightening labor market, have demolished the

odds of easier money in 2026. Short- and long-term Treasury yields rose sharply last week
in the wake of the S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index (PMI)
which pointed to the strongest business expansion in more than five years and the fastest
employment growth in over four years, while also reinforcing expectations that the Fed
will maintain a restrictive policy stance for longer. Markets were expecting two to three
quarter-point rate cuts in 2026; instead, the Fed has already raised rates once this year and

is expected to keep raising rates over the near term.
Shock 3: The Earnings Boom No One Saw Coming. Not all the shocks of 2026 are
negative. Indeed, stronger-than-expected corporate earnings have been among the most
positive factors of this year. Corporate America entered the year facing questions about
tariffs, AI-related capital expenditure spending, the health of the U.S. consumer, operating
leverage and the U.S. dollar outlook. At the start of the year, Wall Street was expecting

roughly $290 of S&P 500 earnings per share for 2026, per Bloomberg estimates. But by
September 21, the estimate had risen by $50, to $340 per share.
Investment Implications
While some of these shocks may
contribute to periods of market
volatility, the combination of strong
earnings growth, robust domestic
demand, and ongoing capital
investment suggests that the
fundamental backdrop for Equities
remains constructive.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Earnings have been upgraded and underpinned by several factors, including robust final 
domestic demand in the U.S., a pickup in global growth, AI-related capital spending, 
productivity gains, and enhanced operating leverage. All these variables have helped deliver 
an earnings “shock” to Wall Street. And as an aside, yes, headline profit growth has been 
inflated by strong technology earnings and one-off investment gains from Amazon and 
Alphabet. That said, the S&P 500’s earnings story remains extraordinary. According to 
FactSet, the 493 companies excluding the Magnificent 7 1 
posted 31.8% earnings growth in 
Q2, the strongest level since late 2021.

Shock 4: An Economy with more Horsepower than the Headlines Suggest. That 
corporate profits have exceeded expectations this ye
ar is due in no small part to an economy 
that continues to surprise to the upside. The Fed was supposed to cut rates this year to 
keep the economy humming and prevent it from losing steam. But rather than limp through 
2026, the economy has leaped ahead. Yes, real gross domestic product (GDP) growth of 
2.1% in Q1 and 1.5% in Q2 are hardly blockbuster annualized rates of growth. However, the 
figures belie the underlying strength of the U.S. economy, with real final domestic demand

(which strips out trade) running at a 4.2% annual rate in Q2. According to the latest reading 
of the Atlanta Fed GDP tracker, Q3 real GDP grow
th is running at a red-hot rate of 5.1%. 
Slowdown? What slowdown?
Resilient consumer spending, robust capital investment, record leve
ls of new business 
formation, and strong foreign direct investment inflows have combined to stoke growth 
in the $32 trillion behemoth called the U.S. economy—the most resilient and dynamic 
economy in the world.
Shock 5: The Unexpected Washington Reset. The last shock is still in the making, but 
given current trends, the odds are rising that the Democrats could control both the House 
and Senate follow
ing the midterm elections. To this point, in January, and per Kalshi, the 
Democrats had a 71% shot at the House and an 18% chance of taking the Senate. Fast-
forward to today, and the odds are a 90% chance that the Democrats take the House and a 
60% chance that they take the Senate.
How all of this plays out remains to be seen—there are some 36 days before the November 
vote, and anything can happen. The market base case remains that the Democrats take the 
House, and the Senate is a toss-up—or too close to call. But with the Democrats expected to 
control at least one chamber of Congress, inv
estors can expect more volatility and uncertainty 
about negotiations ov
er the budget, debt ceiling, defense spending and AI regulation. Another 
political wildcard to watch: the changing dynamics within the Democratic Party.
The Biggest Shock of All for 2026? Pe
rhaps it’s not $6 diesel, rising interest rates, 
solid growth, booming profits or shifting politics. Rather, it’s the ability of the U.S. 
economy to absorb the unexpected, not buckle under stress, and continue generating  wealth 
for investors. To wit, the S&P 500 is on pace for its fourth
 consecutive year of double-digit 
returns.

MARKET VIEW
2026 Has, So Far, Defied the Traditional Midterm Year Playbook
Jordy Fuentes, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Current Landscape. To no surprise, market volatility persisted in 2026. Performance initially
tracked historical midterm election patterns before diverging meaningfully as the year
progressed, though there are still three months to go. Several items remain at hand as U.S.

voters head to the polls in November, including geopolitical and foreign policy uncertainty,
energy driven inflation pressures, affordability concerns, U.S. fiscal trajectory, and rapid
advancements in AI. Regardless of the eventual political outcome, history suggests that
markets and the economy can perform well across different configurations of government.
In our view, markets continue to be anchored by strong fundamentals and encouraging
seasonal trends.

To Be Addressed. U.S. domestic and foreign policy remain in focus as the global reorder,
tit-for-tat tariffs, and geopolitical conflicts spanning the Middle East to Eastern Europe have
added layers of uncertainty to the U.S. economic outlook.
At the same time, persistent geopolitical flare-ups have pushed oil prices materially
higher, adding to inflation pressures and keeping them above the Fed’s 2% target.
Headline Consumer Price Index (CPI) held above 3% year-over-year (YoY) in August, while

its core measure, which excludes food and energy, held at 2.4% YoY. The latest Personal
Consumption Expenditure (PCE), the Fed’s preferred measure of inflation, also remained
above 3% YoY for both its headline and core figures. 2
The U.S. fiscal trajectory is another area of focus. The U.S. budget deficit stands at 5.4% of
GDP , just above this century’s average of 4.6%. Meanwhile, U.S. public sector debt surpassed
the $40 trillion mark for the first time in August, oscillating around 100% of GDP .3 While these

trends warrant close monitoring, we view them as a slow-moving structural headwind rather
than an immediate concern, particularly within a highly innovative and diverse U.S. economy.
AI has emerged as a policy consideration, with debates increasingly centered on both
regulatory safeguards and the economic implications of the expanding infrastructure buildout.
For voters, those concerns extend to affordability, as the significant energy requirements of

new data centers raise questions about potential pressure on electricity costs.
Strap In for The Ride. Leading up to the midterm election, markets tend to experience
greater swings as investors navigate uncertainty surrounding the policy path for the
remainder of the presidential cycle (Exhibit 2A). Since 1930, the S&P 500 has averaged an
intrayear drawdown of 20% in midterm years, with the latest election in 2022 experiencing
a 25% intrayear pullback. This year, however, the S&P 500 largest peak-to-trough drawdown

has been 9%, considerably shallower than its long-term average (Exhibit 2B). Yet while
volatility may rise as election day approaches, 2026 has increasingly defied the traditional
midterm year playbook.
Why 2026 is Different. Despite entering a challenging stretch of the presidential cycle,
U.S. Equities have materially outpaced the typical year two pattern (Exhibit 2C). The S&P
500 experienced its best Q2 in a midterm year since 1938, surging 14.9% compared to an

average decline of 1.6%. From a monthly perspective, September stands out as the weakest
month, though month-to-date performance points to an unusual 1.0% rise. That resilience
follows a 2.6% gain in August, another month in which performance meaningfully exceeded
the historical decline of 0.5% (Exhibit 2D).
Through September, U.S. Equity resilience has also extended beyond headline returns with
both the frequency and magnitude of market pullbacks remaining relatively subdued. Market

corrections of 10% or more have occurred in just 29% of midterm election years since 1930.4
Against this backdrop, the market’s last correction came in spring 2025, while this year has
2 Bloomberg, Bureau of Labor Statistics. Data through August 31, 2026.
3 U.S. Treasury, Bureau of Economic Analysis. Data as of September 21, 2026.
4 Bloomberg, data through December 31, 2022.
Investment Implications
In our view, any related market
weakness could represent a

potential buying opportunity for
long-term investors.

### Page 5

5 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
seen only one 5%+ pullback compared to the average three per year, underscoring relatively
resilient market conditions.5 Year-to-date, the S&P 500 has increased 13%, well above the
average annual return of 3% according to Bloomberg through September 25, and on pace
to mark the strongest midterm election year since 2006. Taken together, 2026 has thus far
defied the traditionally weak midterm year seasonal pattern, reinforcing that every election

cycle is unique and historical trends may be a useful guide to navigate market volatility.
What’s Ahead? Forward performance is increasingly constructive for Equities. In the
post-war period, the S&P 500 rose 100% of the time in the six and 12 months following
elections, averaging 13% and 14%, respectively. 6 Even narrowing the time frame to three
months, markets ended higher 90% of the time. As investors push through the finish line,
the last three months of the year tend to be the strongest with October standing out,

ending higher 67% of the time. On average, Q4 has gained 5.6% since 1930, far surpassing
the preceding three quarters of election years. 7 Greater clarity about the U.S. fiscal policy
path serves as a potential tailwind for investors following the election.
To Take Home. Volatility tends to rise during midterm years, but history suggests that
reacting to short-term political uncertainty has rarely rewarded long-term investors. Over
longer horizons, the data suggests Equity returns and economic strength are shaped less

by election outcomes than by corporate profits, valuations, labor market conditions and
capital investments.

THE TICKER TAPE
What is the bond market telling us?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
We believe the bond market is telling us that the Fed is on track to meeting their dual
mandate of price stability and full employment. More importantly, when examining the
full yield curve the 2-year Treasury yield is currently around 4.88%, which typically is
the most sensitive to the fed funds rate and tends to price in future potential policy

adjustments. At the same time, the 10-year Treasury yield is currently about 5.11%. This
leads us to believe that the market expects the Fed to engineer a couple of more hikes
while the back end of the curve is indicating that economic growth is still healthy.
Taken together, it appears the full curve, which has clearly flattened, believes the Fed is
doing its job, while supply shocks, for obvious reasons, are not leading to higher long-run
inflation. The backup in long-dated yields is growth, not necessarily inflation as indicated

by rising “real” yields. Therefore, we view the drift higher in yields as a more normal sign
than what many have originally suggested. We continue to emphasize higher economic
growth for longer, rising productivity in the medium term, supply shortages to remain in
key areas within the AI space, and shocks in the energy equation. We expect the energy
shocks to ultimately fade away over time, but we do not believe that growth fades
anytime soon. In our view, a more normal bond market pricing in Fed moves at the short

end, and growth at the long end helps financial conditions and economic growth operate
in a more durable manner.
In summary, the front-end of the curve expects 3 to 4 more hikes in the next 18 months
or so. This can be debated obviously based on future data especially short-term energy
supply shocks. Long-end yields are drifting higher based on five main factors below, in
our view.
1. Strong economic growth (both
services and manufacturing)
2. Hawkish Fed

3. Debt and deficit concerns
4. Lower demand for long duration
sovereign debt in general
5. Energy supply shocks
The largest weight being strong economic activity which kick starts portfolio
repositioning particularly as the Fed hikes rates. The repositioning stops once demand
rises again when yields reach attractive levels for the more patient longer term
investment community. The supply shocks ultimately end and debt and deficit worries will
likely remain, but they still take a backseat for now. Longer duration government debt is

becoming more attractive each week even in the face of the concerns above. Be patient

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

S&P 500 Index Price Return is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States. A price return index is a stock
market index that tracks only the price changes and capital gains or losses of its component securities, without including dividends or cash distributions.
S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index is an early monthly economic indicator that estimates overall private-sector business activity by combining manufacturing

Investing involves risk, including the possible loss of principal. Past performance is no guarantee of future results.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and
investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.

Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

► 伊朗支持的葉門胡塞武裝組織持續攻擊沙烏
地阿 拉 伯 和荷 姆 茲 海峽 船 隻，美 期 中 選舉
前，地緣政治風險不減 ，添股市賣壓 。中國
長鑫存儲在新型晶片研發取得進展 ，衝擊半
導體類股 ，美光與三星 、海力士記憶體廠即
使仍供不應求 ，亦遭市場拋售 ，南韓股指週
跌幅逾 18% 。 網 通 晶 片 大 廠 博 通 執 行 長 表

示：資金是 AI建設擴張面臨的最大瓶頸 ，光
通訊股領跌走弱，科技、半導體股重挫。
► 美股第三季財報登場 ，花旗、高盛本季淨利
優於預期 ，但富國銀行淨利息收入失色 ，摩
根大通自 2023年4Q以來 ，首次獲利不如預
期，美銀亦不佳 。艾斯摩爾上修全年營收展
望，透露部分客戶已洽詢2029年至2030年設
備訂單，顯示晶片擴產需求仍強 。台積電3Q
毛利率達66.4%較前月略減，因2奈米快速放
量與海外廠產能利用率造成稀釋 ，但今年資
本支出財測維持600到640億美元不變，反映
代理AI客戶需求強勁 ，預期2026年至2028年
資本支出將較過去三年進一步顯著提高。

► 美國 9 月CPI 、PPI 皆上升 ， 且 超 過 市 場 預
期，9月PPI月增率為 1.3%，創2022年來最
大漲幅，高於市場預期的月增 0.5%，年增率
達6.3%，為2022年12月來最大漲幅 ，顯示
通膨壓力正從能源擴大至其他領域 。通膨僵
固性使市場預期高利率將維持更久時間 ，美
國公債殖利率一度走高 ，但隨股市重挫 、避
險資金轉入 ，公債止跌回升 。體質較弱的企
業債出現警訊 ，CoreWeave債券殖利率大幅
攀升 ， 2032 年 到 期 債 券 殖 利 率 一 度 升 至
15%，反應公司舉債擴建資料中心 ，市場擔
憂其債 務規 模 、 利 息負擔 及未來 再融 資能
力，除違約風險上升 ，過高成本侵蝕企業獲
利、股債皆挫 ，顯示目前即使是 AI、科技信
用債表現也開始出現分化。
► 中東地緣風險推升油價上漲 ，儘管川普宣布

將暫時放寬對俄羅斯柴油出口的限制 ，西德
州油價仍處於每桶 98美元高位 ，推升全球通
膨預期 。虛擬資產比特幣 、以太幣隨風險性
資產同步下挫。

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至 3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由 2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率 0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因 ，
貢獻整體 CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導 ，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性 ，需求面
通膨略有提高 ，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在 4%以上維持更久 。受
到油價高盤 、通膨僵固 ，與財政惡化 、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響 ，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中 ， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈 ，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大 、
歐元走弱 ，美元轉強 ，商品、黃金承壓 。法
國政治 不確定性讓 歐股修正 、法股最疲 ，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高 ，增加企業借貸成本

與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。
熱門議題
熱門議題
美歐等成熟國家近期公債殖利率迅速彈升美國通膨仍具僵固性，高油價仍是主因

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，Goldman Sachs，凱基證券整理，註：右上圖之大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)企業為Amazon、Alphabet、Microsoft、Oracle
► 美國第三季財報季展開 ，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現 。根據FactSet數據
顯示 ，標普500指數第三季獲利 成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐 ，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI 需求帶動 ， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股 ，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力 。高盛指出 ，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普 500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長 ，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響 ，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間 ，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面 。隨財報
數據陸續公布 ，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現 ，並維持正向財測 ，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價 ，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐
熱門議題
美股第三季獲利成長持續上修，科技為主要支撐 CSP雲端營收加速，資本支出變現跡象持續受驗證
評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張
熱門議題財報展望

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長
投資焦點
醫療生技

### Page 8

8
內部參考文件請勿外流
► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

投資焦點
近3個月漲幅和預估獲利呈正成長，醫療生技股佳
醫療保健和生技預估獲利明年明顯成長，有利股價續揚
資料來源：Bloomberg，凱基投顧，凱基證券整理
預估今明年產業獲利差異，醫療保健和生技成長最大

### Page 9

9
內部參考文件請勿外流
股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金

融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。

### Page 17

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。
